/**
 * ============================================================================
 * ORBIT Engine v2
 * Author: Icehellionx
 * Role: Relation / Power CNN classifier for RP chatbots.
 *
 * High-level:
 *   - Loads a quantized CNN model from getRawModelData().
 *   - Tokenizes the latest user message.
 *   - Runs a forward pass through:
 *       * Embedding layer
 *       * 1D CNN with kernels [3, 4, 5] + max-pool + ReLU
 *       * Two softmax heads:
 *           - Relation (primary)
 *           - Power    (secondary)
 *   - Writes a readable debug line into context.character.scenario.
 *
 * NOTE: This version is wired to the Int8 export that uses:
 *         weights: emb, c0w, c0b, c1w, c1b, c2w, c2b, rel_w, rel_b, pow_w, pow_b
 *         scales:  same keys as weights
 *         labels: { relation: [...], power: [...] }
 *       and expects getRawModelData() to return a JSON STRING.
 * ============================================================================
 */

(function () {
    'use strict';

    // ========================================================================
    // GLOBAL CONFIG
    // ========================================================================
    var DEBUG = 0;         // 1 = log [ORBIT] lines to console
    var MAX_TOKENS = 60;   // Must match Python trainer sequence length

    // ========================================================================
    // SAFE LOGGING
    // ========================================================================
    function safeLog(msg) {
        try {
            if (console && console.log) console.log(msg);
        } catch (e) {}
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // MODEL INIT
    // ========================================================================
    var MODEL = null;
    var MODEL_INIT_ERROR = false;

    function initModel() {
        if (MODEL || MODEL_INIT_ERROR) return;

        var raw = "";
        try {
            raw = getRawModelData();   // MUST return a JSON STRING
        } catch (e) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            safeLog("[ORBIT] getRawModelData() threw: " + e.message);
            return;
        }

        if (!raw || typeof raw !== "string" || raw.length < 10) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            safeLog("[ORBIT] Model: Empty or invalid raw data.");
            return;
        }

        var parsed;
        try {
            parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        } catch (e) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            safeLog("[ORBIT] JSON parse error: " + e.message);
            return;
        }

        try {
            var weights = parsed.weights || {};
            var scales  = parsed.scales  || {};
            var meta    = parsed.meta    || {};
            var labels  = parsed.labels  || {};

            // Dequantize int8 -> float32 using a scalar scale
            function deq(vals, scale) {
                if (!vals || scale == null) return null;
                var out = new Float32Array(vals.length);
                for (var i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
                    out[i] = vals[i] * scale;
                }
                return out;
            }

            // Convolution kernels [3, 4, 5]
            var convs = [];
            var K = [3, 4, 5];

            for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                convs.push({
                    w: deq(weights["c" + i + "w"], scales["c" + i + "w"]),
                    b: deq(weights["c" + i + "b"], scales["c" + i + "b"]),
                    k: K[i]
                });
            }

            // Build vocabulary map
            var vocabMap = {};
            var vocabArr = Array.isArray(parsed.vocab) ? parsed.vocab : [];
            for (var j = 0; j < vocabArr.length; j++) {
                vocabMap[vocabArr[j]] = j;
            }

            var unkId = (vocabMap["<unk>"] !== undefined) ? vocabMap["<unk>"] : 0;

            var relationLabels = labels.relation || [];
            var powerLabels    = labels.power    || [];

            if (!relationLabels.length || !powerLabels.length) {
                safeLog("[ORBIT] WARNING: Empty relation or power labels; outputs may be blank.");
            }

            MODEL = {
                vocabMap: vocabMap,
                unkId: unkId,
                vocabSize: vocabArr.length,
                embedDim: (meta.embed || parsed.embed_dim || 24),

                // Conv layers
                convs: convs,
                embedding: deq(weights.emb, scales.emb),

                // Relation head (single-label softmax)
                relW: deq(weights["rel_w"], scales["rel_w"]),
                relB: deq(weights["rel_b"], scales["rel_b"]),

                // Power head (single-label softmax)
                powW: deq(weights["pow_w"], scales["pow_w"]),
                powB: deq(weights["pow_b"], scales["pow_b"]),

                relationLabels: relationLabels,
                powerLabels: powerLabels,

                numRel: relationLabels.length,
                numPow: powerLabels.length
            };

            safeLog(
                "[ORBIT] Init success. Vocab: " +
                MODEL.vocabSize +
                ", Model Type: " + (parsed.model_type || "cnn_orbit_int8_v1")
            );
        } catch (e) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            safeLog("[ORBIT] Init failed: " + e.message);
        }
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // TOKENIZER (mirror Python as closely as possible)
    // ========================================================================
    function cnn_tokenize(text) {
        return (text || "")
            .toLowerCase()
            // keep [a-z0-9 whitespace []], nuke the rest
            .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s\[\]]/g, " ")
            .trim()
            .split(/\s+/)
            .filter(Boolean)
            .slice(0, MAX_TOKENS);
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // CONV1D MAX + RELU
    // ========================================================================
    function conv1d_max_relu(inputCHW, weight, bias, C, L, k) {
        if (!weight || !bias) return new Float32Array(0);

        var O = (weight.length / (C * k)) | 0;
        var out = new Float32Array(O);
        var pad = (k - 1) >> 1;

        for (var f = 0; f < O; f++) {
            var maxVal = -Infinity;
            var wOff = f * C * k;

            for (var t = 0; t < L; t++) {
                var s = bias[f];

                for (var c = 0; c < C; c++) {
                    var baseInput = c * L;
                    var baseWeight = wOff + c * k;

                    for (var j = 0; j < k; j++) {
                        var idx = t + j - pad;
                        if (idx >= 0 && idx < L) {
                            s += inputCHW[baseInput + idx] * weight[baseWeight + j];
                        }
                    }
                }

                var relu = s > 0 ? s : 0;
                if (relu > maxVal) maxVal = relu;
            }

            out[f] = maxVal;
        }

        return out;
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // CNN FORWARD PASS
    // ========================================================================
    function cnn_predict(text) {
        initModel();
        if (!MODEL) return null;

        var tokens = cnn_tokenize(text);
        var ids = [];

        for (var i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
            var t = tokens[i];
            ids.push(MODEL.vocabMap[t] !== undefined ? MODEL.vocabMap[t] : MODEL.unkId);
        }
        while (ids.length < MAX_TOKENS) ids.push(0);

        var C = MODEL.embedDim;
        var T = MAX_TOKENS;

        // Embedding lookup -> CHW (C x T)
        var embedded = new Float32Array(C * T);
        for (var t = 0; t < T; t++) {
            var id = ids[t];
            var off = id * C;
            for (var c = 0; c < C; c++) {
                embedded[c * T + t] = MODEL.embedding[off + c];
            }
        }

        // Convs + global max-pool
        var feats = [];
        var total = 0;
        for (var k = 0; k < MODEL.convs.length; k++) {
            var cv = MODEL.convs[k];
            var res = conv1d_max_relu(embedded, cv.w, cv.b, C, T, cv.k);
            feats.push(res);
            total += res.length;
        }

        var flat = new Float32Array(total);
        var ptr = 0;
        for (var f = 0; f < feats.length; f++) {
            flat.set(feats[f], ptr);
            ptr += feats[f].length;
        }

        function forwardHead(W, B, n) {
            if (!W || !B || !n) return new Float32Array(0);

            var logits = new Float32Array(n);

            for (var r = 0; r < n; r++) {
                var sum = B[r];
                var base = r * total;
                for (var q = 0; q < total; q++) {
                    sum += W[base + q] * flat[q];
                }
                logits[r] = sum;
            }

            var out = new Float32Array(n);
            var maxL = -Infinity;
            for (var i2 = 0; i2 < n; i2++) {
                if (logits[i2] > maxL) maxL = logits[i2];
            }

            var sumExp = 0;
            for (var j2 = 0; j2 < n; j2++) {
                out[j2] = Math.exp(logits[j2] - maxL);
                sumExp += out[j2];
            }

            if (!sumExp) sumExp = 1;
            for (var k2 = 0; k2 < n; k2++) {
                out[k2] /= sumExp;
            }

            return out;
        }

        return {
            rel: forwardHead(MODEL.relW, MODEL.relB, MODEL.numRel),
            pow: forwardHead(MODEL.powW, MODEL.powB, MODEL.numPow)
        };
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // RUNTIME INTEGRATION
    // ========================================================================
    if (typeof context !== "object" || !context) return;

    context.character = context.character || {};
    context.character.scenario = (typeof context.character.scenario === "string")
        ? context.character.scenario
        : "";
    context.character.example_dialogues = context.character.example_dialogues || "";

    var chat = context.chat || {};
    var lastMessage = chat.last_message || "";
    var msgs = Array.isArray(chat.last_messages) ? chat.last_messages : [];
    var msgCount = typeof chat.message_count === "number"
        ? chat.message_count
        : (msgs.length || (lastMessage ? 1 : 0));

    // Helper: format top-2 labels
    function fmtTopTwo(labels, arr) {
        if (!labels || !labels.length || !arr || !arr.length) return "";
        var pairs = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) {
            pairs.push({ label: labels[i], prob: arr[i] });
        }
        pairs.sort(function (a, b) { return b.prob - a.prob; });
        var top = pairs.slice(0, 2);
        return top.map(function (p) {
            return p.label + ":" + Math.round(p.prob * 100) + "%";
        }).join(", ");
    }

    // Helper: run model on text and append a labeled ORBIT line
    function runAndAppendOrbit(prefix, text) {
        if (!text || !text.trim()) return;
        try {
            var res = cnn_predict(text);
            if (res && MODEL) {
                var relStr = fmtTopTwo(MODEL.relationLabels, res.rel);
                var powStr = fmtTopTwo(MODEL.powerLabels, res.pow);

                var prefixStr = prefix ? (prefix + " ") : "";
                var line = "[ORBIT]: " + prefixStr + "Relation: " + relStr + " | Power: " + powStr + "[/ORBIT]";

                if (DEBUG) safeLog(line);
                context.character.scenario += line;
            }
        } catch (err) {
            safeLog("[ORBIT ERROR] " + err.message);
        }
    }

    // 1) Single-shot on the current last_message
    if (lastMessage) {
        runAndAppendOrbit("Current:", lastMessage);
    }

    // 2) Aggregate over bot messages in history:
    //    - Prefer entries where isUser === false
    //    - Fallback: odd-numbered turns (2nd, 4th, 6th, ... => index 1,3,5,...) so it tracks bot turns.
    if (msgs.length > 0) {
        var botPieces = [];

        for (var i = 0; i < msgs.length; i++) {
            var m = msgs[i] || {};
            var text = m.message || m.content || "";

            // Heuristic: "bot" if explicitly marked, OR if it's an even turn (1-based index 2,4,6,...)
            var isBotByFlag = (m.isUser === false);
            var isBotByIndex = ((i + 1) % 2 === 0); // 2nd, 4th, 6th... from message_count/history

            if ((isBotByFlag || isBotByIndex) && text && text.trim()) {
                botPieces.push(text.trim());
            }
        }

        var historyText = botPieces.join(" ");
        if (historyText) {
            runAndAppendOrbit("HistoryBots:", historyText);
        }
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // RAW MODEL DATA (STRING-BASED, SECURITY-SAFE)
    // ========================================================================
    // IMPORTANT:
    //  - This MUST return a STRING.
    //  - Paste your minified JSON inside the string literal below.
    function getRawModelData() {
        return [
            // Replace the empty string with your full JSON (single line or minified).
            // Example:
            // '{"model_type":"cnn_orbit_int8_v1","vocab":["<pad>","<unk>",...],"labels":{"relation":[...],"power":[...]},...}'
            '{"model_type":"cnn_orbit_int8_v1","meta":{"strategy":"adaptive","filters":36,"embed":48},"vocab":["<pad>","<unk>","the",",",".","as","to","a","of","with","and","you","while","in","[rolee]","[rolea]","[roled]","[roleb]","[rolec]","their","they","on","for","them","i","at","when","your","it","asks","if","into","down","that","out","from","is","up","room","says","during","smell","time","me","-","off","back","air","last","sound","away","without","door","like","before","quiet","can","around","not","tells","but","this","theyre","youre","through","its","heavy","[roled]s","hands","just","over","shut","[rolec]s","[roleb]s","silence","[rolee]s","so","only","next","[rolea]s","hand","table","past","against","slowly","turns","between","shoulder","feel","walk","nearby","he","by","an","under","leans","she","dont","?","street","are","faint","soft","one","my","checks","morning","distant","closer","glass","lets","conversation","hour","quietly","others","both","same","have","something","joke","fills","what","window","keeps","someone","behind","loudly","sharp","toward","how","close","laughs","begins","eyes","floor","wait","do","head","until","every","everyone","waiting","moment","side","still","near","talk","taps","distance","about","check","say","wind","slow","phone","softly","stand","hum","im","train","open","together","light","gives","keep","kettle","hums","rain","line","lights","looks","be","sky","scent","follows","then","too","being","dark","break","old","shifts","night","look","leaves","buzzes","points","steps","each","first","makes","blanket","see","once","even","shakes","moves","breeze","settles","buzz","long","voices","sit","make","finally","sign","beside","more","sun","than","early","gear","making","fades","across","dog","barks","comment","anyone","want","speaks","music","crosswalk","hallway","rests","laugh","ice","turning","brings","way","whenever","never","smile","overhead","after","get","sharply","hangs","outside","chair","share","low","lightly","things","which","all","embe rhythmically","will","sentence","was","belongings","knee","color","bruised","peaches","drink","library","fan","wobbles","shared","youd","chatter","static","filled","books","usual","rages","roof","holding","youve","plan","church","bells","helps","receipt","try","ozone","howls","grandfather","driver","grin","beats","good","exhaust","fumes","uneasy","echoes","memory","watching","zero","vending","dispenses","thud","dampness","cellar","creeping","does","sudden","draft","chills","dawn","leave","candles","burn","shapeless","nubs","lull","siren","tight","runs","parade","pen","sets","player","skips","scratch","credits","rolling","has","word","struggle","breaths","theyll","rummages","drawer","dishwasher","cycle","clatters","gesture","touching","deliberately","doors","floorboards","groan","shifting","patterns","realize","counter","stiffly","posture","construction","buildings","few","isnt","folds","streetlights","sleeve","conditioner","steadily","done","reply","arrive","uses","suggestion","nobody","bumps","throw","torn","actually","jammed","polite","ticks","should","ticket","cat","varnish","radiator","hissing","secret","pressing","start","gutter","overflows","noisily","saying","challenges","said","briefly","menacing","shade","gray","taking","flutters","sadly","crashing","notes","follow","explain","answering","frost","delicate","list","clerk","wouldnt","soup","bubbles","stove","scoff","screech","return","swings","squeaks","snowflakes","scared","barista","fluorescent","reads","would","item","agrees","almost","stinging","warmly","worth","grounding","idea","refuses","coffee","autumn","rustle","cheek","tired","fine","offering","stretch","finish","any","mid","trade","items","weaves","turn","surprised","applause","murmur","immediately","angles","spare","slides","horn","trusting","snow","plow","scrapes","speaking","onto","changes","ahead","please","covers","longer","pulls","got","smirks","brakes","wont","chaotic","hotel","stack","lifts","desk","forward","remember","care","sorry","pats"
